[CA] Consideration of Bid #S -15-22 for a Contract between the City of Cheyenne and Great 
Western Installations for the synthetic turf safety surfacing for Romero Park and Sun Valley Park 
Playgrounds Project (Development Impact Fees and Land and Water Conservation Grants) – 
referred to Finance Committee. 
 
 One available retail liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office by Ace’s 
Range LLC d/b/a Ace’s Range LLC, 1505 Pioneer Ave., Big D Oil Co. d/b/a Big D Oil #56, 4100 
Whitney Rd., Billy Jacks - Wyoming LLC d/b/a Billy Jack’s Pizza Pub, 4020 Airport Parkway 
West, Suite 1, Capitol Cuisine LLC d/b/a Ike’s Bar & Grill, 122 W. 6 th St., Ful-O-Pep d/b/a The 
Old Cheyenne Elevator, 800 W. 21st St., Get Bent, LLC d/b/a Get Bent, LLC, 707 W. Lincolnway,  
Peaches’ Package Liquor & Bar LLC d/b/a Peaches’ Package Liquor & Bar LLC, Anderson 
Corner, 3 rd Filing, Lot 2, Block 1, Vitamin Co ttage Natural Food Markets, Inc. d/b/a Natural 
Grocers, 1851 Dell Range Blvd., Westby Edge LLC d/b/a Westby Edge LLC, 714 W. 20th St. and 
Wyoming Downs OTB 14 LLC d/b/a Wyoming Downs OTB 14, 1345 Dell Range Blvd., 
Cheyenne.  Mayor announced Billy Jack’s  Pizza Pub has withdrawn their application  and has 
submitted an application for a bar & grill license and announced public comments will be limited 
to 3 minutes.  Seth Stefanik, co -owner of Los Conejos and Micro Popup Concepts, provided 
background information on the Los Conejos food truck explaining it was a very successful startup 
business during Covid. He provided information on the “ghost kitchen ” concept noting it is a  
professional food preparation and c ooking facility for delivery -only meals and doesn’t include a 
storefront or indoor seating. The concept also includes QR codes posted around town that can be 
accessed for ghost kitchen information including how to place orders and information on deliveries 
and pickups.  Darrin Westby, Westby Edge LLC,  expressed appreciation to the governing body 
for consideration of their application  and also their consideration for the resolution addressing 
stifling state statutes involving liquor licenses  in hopes that le gislative changes are made.  Mr. 
Westby advised he a ppreciates focus on blighted areas through town and acknowledged council 
members were placed in an unfortunate situation, but applicants were also put in a difficult position 
as they were competing agains t friends.  April Brimmer Kunz, Aces Range  LLC, echoed Mr. 
Westby’s concerns with antiquated liquor laws and expressed appreciation to the governing body 
for their efforts to initiate change.  Ms. Brimmer Kunz advised t hey will proceed with opening 
their business and continue to work to bring logic to the liquor licensing process and that noted 
she fully intends to engage as a lobbyist for not only Aces Range LLC but also for the city and 
state.  She noted that she had learned there are 55 available licenses in the county and expressed 
support for Get Bent, LLC.  Mayor Collins explained the 55 county licenses can’t be used within 
5 miles of city limits so they would have to be utilized in rural areas.  Chad Willett, Get Bent , 
LLC, advised they have applied for a pre-application meeting that is scheduled for this week with 
the Planning and Development Department for conditional use.   Mr. Willett noted they w ill 
continue to stand with colleagues for reform of  antiquated liquor license requirements moving 
forward and applauded members of the governing body for their resolution addressing stifling state 
statutes involving liquor licenses.  Jen Seitz, The Old Cheyenne Elevator, spoke in support of all 
projects, voiced concern regarding reverting the license based on a timeline as it may be difficult 
to achieve.  She spoke about the Metropolitan and how they opened a craft liquor store while the 
remainder of the business was  being completed and noted 7 months is a very tight timeframe to 
achieve a certificate of occupancy due to supply chain and other factors.  Ms. Seitz referenced how 
long it took the Municipal Building to reopen to the public due to supply chain issues and advised 
state statutes outline a timeframe in which businesses have to become operational and suggested 
there not be any additional restrictions added to the license.  Mr. Roybal moved to approve Get 
Bent, LLC d/b/a Get Bent, LLC, 707 W. Lincolnway, with the following condition: “if the license 
does not receive a full certificate of occupancy by December 31, 2022, the license reverts in whole 
to the City of Cheyenne”, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Upon inquiry, City Attorney Stefanie Boster 
explained a full certificate of occupancy would req uire that the F ire Marshall and B uilding 
Department inspect and approve, all pertinent city codes are met including the planning process 
with a site plan.  The following comments were made related to the application for Get Bent, LLC: 
applicant does not meet the guidelines of restoring a blighted building , concerns of the business 
fully utilizing the license in regards to a full service restaurant, and very limited space for package 
store, dining, bar etc. , timeline provided by Planning and Development Director Charles Bloom 
indicates it will be a challenge for the  business to meet the conditions timeline and opinion that 
Westby Edge is closer to meeting all re quirements.  Upon inquiry, Mayor Collins advised the  
governing body would have  the ability to extend  the deadline.  City Clerk Kris Jones further 
explained the timeline extension would  go back before  the g overning body for approval . Ms. 
Boster advised the governing body is currently  awarding the liquor license and the license won’t 
be issued until a full certificate of occupancy is received, noting  the council has full authority to 
bring the license back before the governing body for considerati on of a timeline extension.  
Discussion continued and included: support for the Get Bent , LLC concept downtown, available 
liquor license options for other applicants, difficulty meeting timeframe requirement, request to 
exercise grace while working with the licensee regarding the timeframe requirement, and request 
that any anticipated delays be shared with the council.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body with the exception of Mayor Collins and Mr. Segrave voting “no”.   
 
Upon inquiry, Robin Lockman, City Treasurer provided information on a voucher payable 
to Christa McDonald for volunteer hours for the Foster Grandparent program noting federal funds 
are received for the program and distributed to the program volunteers by the c ity.  Dr. Aldrich 
provided further information advising it is a pass-through program and volunteers receive minimal 
compensation.  Vicki Nemecek, upon inquiry, provided information on a voucher payable to 
Cheryl Y. Tottenhoff Living Trust noting the Street and Alley rental is across the street from their 
facility and is used to store sweepers.  No further comments were made on the Voucher Report as 
presented. 
 
No comments were made on the Revenue Report as presented. 
 
Appointment of Robert C. Jarosh as City Hearing Officer for the City of Cheyenne.  Dr. 
Aldrich moved to approve, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body with the exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote (mayoral 
appointment). 
 
Appointment of Brian Eicholtz to the International Fire Code Board of Appeals.  Mr. 
Segrave moved to approve, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body with the exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote (mayoral 
appointment). 
 
Reappointments of Kyle Wall and Lane Pilbin to the International Fire Code Board of 
Appeals.  Mr. Segrave moved to approve, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” 
– all members of the g overning body with the exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote 
(mayoral appointment).

to determine whether compliance of annexation conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15 -1-
402 exist for 2600 Missile Drive (located east of I-25, north of Missile Drive) 
 
 Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held May 9, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
to determine whether compliance of annexation conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15 -1-
402 exist for Saddle Ridge, 18th Filing (located northwest of the intersection of Christensen Road 
and Pershing Boulevard). 
 
 Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held May 9, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
to determine whether compliance of annexation conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15 -1-
402 exist for a portion of the S1/2 of Section 27, T.13N., R.67W, of the 6 th PM, Laramie County, 
WY (located west of I-25, north of High Plains Road).   
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held May 9, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting 
for a for a bar and grill license application filed  with the City Clerk’s Office for Billy Jacks – 
Wyoming LLC d/b/a Billy Jack’s Pizza Pub, 4020 Airport Parkway West Suite 1, Cheyenne, WY. 
 
Announcement of a Public Hearing to be held May 9, 2022  at 6:00 p.m. in City Council 
Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting for a for 
a microbrewery license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Blue Raven Brewery, 
LLC, d/b/a Blue Raven Brewery, LLC, 209 E. 18th St., Cheyenne, WY. 
 Under other business:   
 
 Jim Ridgeway inquired on the process of cleaning storm drains.  Vicki Nemecek, Director 
of Public Works, explained the inlet cleaning process which includes one truck and one crew that 
starts downtown and works their way out to clean about 3,000 inlets once a year.  She noted the 
cleanings can only take place when weather cooperates and if there is a particular problematic 
area, a request can be called in for maintenance.  
 
 Upon inquiry by Mr.  Ridgeway, Mayor Collins provided information on outstanding 
parking tickets and how much was collected during the  amnesty period noting outstanding    
parking fees continue to be a challenge that is being addressed.  He further explained that typically 
people who have outstanding tickets are aware they have them and they aren’t paying them.   
 
 Mr. Laybourn advised he recently visited the mobile home park located at 316 Central 
Avenue with the Building and Nuisance department directors.  He explained the situation and 
condition of the mobile home park is out of control with trailer homes that are busted open and 
debris scattered everywhere, noting the property owner isn’t capable of remedying the issue .  He 
expressed the urgency of cleaning up the property, particularly cleaning up debris along Crow 
Creek.  Mr. Laybourn referenced issues with the lien process, trouble with property in the past and 
also with scenarios that involve the city cleaning up the property and not being able to collect 
money. Mayor Collins  advised he a nd the Chief of Staff recently visited the area and they are 
working with staff to start a process to clean up the area. 
 
 Dr. Aldrich noted she had attended the Cheyenne Animal Shelter F ur Ball recently and 
provided information on money raised for the event that will help with the facility and taking care 
of animals in the community. 
 
 Mr. Johnson provided information on 3 upcoming events that benefit Rooted in Cheyenne 
starting Friday, April 29th at Freedom’s Edge, May 7th at Tribe Tattoo and May 14th, which is the 
spring planting day. 
 
 Mr. Roybal moved to meet in executive session  pursuant to Wy oming Statute §16 -4-
405(a)(iii)(vii)(x), seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session,  not open 
to the public; requested the City Attorney, City Clerk, City Treasurer, Chief of Staff and members 
of Planning and Development staff be in attendance and stated there would be no action taken by 
the governing body as a result of the executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting 
would officially conclude at the close of the executive session.  (Clerk’s Note: executive session 
convened at 7:42 p.m.) 
 
 
 Following closure of the executive session, Mayor Collins reconvened the regular meeting 
at 8:29 p.m. 
 
 There being  no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 8:29 p.m.   
 
        Submitted by, 
 
             
  
        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk 
 
This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request. 
 
Published: Wyoming Tribune-Eagle 
  May 4, 2022

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2022-05-09 | CLIP_ID: 849 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 849

  
RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
May 9, 2022 
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich (via Zoom), Bryan Cook, Ken 
Esquibel, Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff 
White. Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
  
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the de signation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. Upon request by Mr. White, and pursuant to support received 
by Mr. Roybal and Mr. Cook, agenda item #’s 36(b), 36(c), 37(c) and 40(b)  were removed from 
the Consent Agenda.   Mr. Roybal moved to approve  remaining items, seconded by Mr. White.  
Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Dr. Rinne 
voting “no”.  
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on April 25, 2022.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
      Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to determine whether compliance of annexation 
conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15 -1-402 exist for 2600 Missile Drive (located east of 
I-25, north of Missile Drive).  Seth Lloyd, Planning and Development Department, provided 
information on the property, noting it is already partially in the city, and reviewed requirements of 
compliance with annexation conditions contained in state statutes .  Upon inquiry , Mr. Lloyd 
advised the current development is a lumber company and the use will be expanded.  Jim Ridgeway 
inquired if the property location is adjacent to or on an  abandoned city landfill.  Charles Bloom, 
Director of Planning and Development, explained that since the property is already developed, the 
location has no bearing on the annexation and if there is any contamination it is the landowner’s 
responsibility.  There being no further comments or input and purpose of the hearing having been 
met, Mayor Collins closed the public hearing. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a p ublic hearing to determine whether compliance of annexation 
conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15 -1-402 exist for Saddle Ridge, 18 th Filing (located 
northwest of the intersection of Christensen Road and Pershing Boulevard). Seth Lloyd, Planning 
and Development Department, provided information on the property, noting it is a continuation of 
the Saddle Ridge development and will include Pershing and Christensen rights-of-way and there 
will be no facilities on the site.   Mr. Lloyd further reviewed requirements of compliance with 
annexation conditions contained in state statutes.  There being no further comments or input and 
purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public hearing. 
 Mayor Collins opened a public to determine whether compliance of annexation conditions 
required by Wyoming Statute §15 -1-402 exist for a portion of the S1/2 of Section 27, T.13N., 
R.67W, of the 6 th PM, Laramie County, WY (located west of I -25, north of High Plains Road).   
Seth Lloyd, Planning and Development Department, provided information on the property, noting 
the property will contain an entertainment facility, and reviewed requirements of compliance with 
annexation conditions contained in state statutes .  There being no further comments or input and 
purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public hearing. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for a bar and grill license application filed with the 
City Clerk’s Office for Billy Jacks – Wyoming LLC d/b/a Billy Jack’s Pizza Pub, 4020 Airport 
Parkway West Suite 1, Cheyenne, WY.   Jerry Cox, Director of Operations for Billy Jack ’s Pizza 
Pub, advised he was available for  questions. Upon inquiry, Mr. Cox explained the  application is 
for their airport location and the application was submitted prior to consideration of the airport 
submitting an application pursuant to recently passed House Bill 0055 for a retail liquor license .  
Mr. Cox noted they have a meeting with the airport director later this week  about submitting an 
application for a retail liquor license.  Support for the business at the airport was expressed.  Upon 
inquiry, Kris Jones, City Clerk, explained the retail liquor license must be applied for by the airport 
and the airport can contract with one entity for food and beverage services.  There being no further 
comments or input and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public 
hearing and referred the application to Finance Committee. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a public  hearing for a microbrewery license application filed with 
the City Clerk’s Office for Blue Raven Brewery, LLC, d/b/a Blue Raven Brewery, LLC, 209 E. 
18th St., Cheyenne, WY.  Richard Hart, Blue Raven Brewery, explained they are in the process of 
acquiring the current Danielmark’s Brewery and advised he was available for questions.  There 
being no further comments or input and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins 
closed the public hearing and referred the application to Finance Committee.

RESOLUTION – Declaring that the Municipalities of Laramie County have agreed that 
the current State Statutes regarding the designation of liquor licenses issued by the governing 
bodies of said municipalities are stifling economic growth, and are requesting the State Legislative 
committees and State Legislature review, consider, and adopt modifications to state statutes 
regarding said licenses.  Mr. White moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Johnson.  Mr. White moved 
to amend by substitute dated May 3, 2022, seco nded by Mr. Segrave. Mayor Collins advised the 
substitute includes the addition of “food trucks” in the seventh “Whereas” paragraph.  Motion to 
amend by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  It was noted that 
food trucks were added since they have become popular since liquor laws were originally written, 
and comment was made that the resolution was left vague to give the legislature flexibility.  Main 
motion as amended by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. 
(#6231)

LLC d/b/a The Office Bar & Eatery, 1600 E. Pershing Blvd. #100, Cheyenne, WY.   Motion (per

No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
 Designation of Official Voting Delegate and Alternate Delegate for the City of Cheyenne 
at the June 9, 2022 Wyoming Association of Municipalities Summer Co nvention Business 
Meeting to be held in Laramie, Wyoming.   Mr. White moved to approve  Councilman Dr. Mark 
Rinne as official voting delegate and Mayor Patrick Collins as alternate voting delegate, seconded 
by Mr. Johnson.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held May 23, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
for a bar & grill liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Westby Edge LLC 
d/b/a Westby Edge LLC, 714 W. 20th St., Cheyenne, WY. 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held May 23, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
for a microbrewery liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Westby Edge 
LLC d/b/a Westby Edge LLC, 714 W. 20th St., Cheyenne, WY. 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held May 23, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
to receive comments and input for the Hynds and the Hole Urban Renewal Plan and Proj ect as 
required by Wyoming Statute pursuant to Wyoming Statute §15-9-109.   
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held May 23, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
to receive comments and input for the 15 th Street Corridor Renewal Plan and Project as required 
by Wyoming Statute pursuant to Wyoming Statute §15-9-109.   
Announcement was made that a Calcutta, benefitting the Black Dog Animal Rescue, will 
be held Friday, June 3, 2022, beginning at 6:00 p.m. at Alf’s Pub and Bar, 1622 E. 19 th Street, 
Cheyenne, Wyoming in conjunction with the 2022 Black Dog Animal Rescue Cornhole 
Tournament (35% of proceeds go to the Black Dog Animal Rescue).   
 Under other business:   
 
 Mr. Johnson provided information on the Rooted in Cheyenne planting event to be held on 
Saturday, May 14, 2022 and applauded Community Recreation and Events staff for the Friday’s 
on the Plaza concert announcement event.   
 
 Mr. Segrave provided information on the Downtown Cleanup event to be held Saturday, 
May 14, 2022 starting at 10 a.m., noting another cleanup will be done before Cheyenne Frontier 
Days. 
 
 Mr. Roybal moved to meet in executive session  pursuant to Wyoming Statute §16 -4-
405(a)(iii)(vii), seconded by Mr. Segrave .  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session,  not open 
to the public; requested the City Attorney, City Clerk, City Treasurer, Chief of Staff and members 
of Planning and Development staff be in attendance and stated there would be no action taken by 
the governing body as a result of the executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting 
would officially conclude at the close of the executive session.  (Clerk’s Note: executive session 
convened at 6:42 p.m.) 
 
 Following closure of the executive session, Mayor Collins reconvened the regular meeting 
at 7:13 p.m. 
 
 There being  no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:13 p.m.   
 
 
 
 
 
        Submitted by, 
 
             
  
        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk 
 
This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request.

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2022-05-23 | CLIP_ID: 851 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 851

  
RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
May 23, 2022 
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Ken Esquibel, Richard Johnson, 
Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave (via Zoom) and Jeff White. Absent: 
Bryan Cook.  Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
 EXPRESSION OF APPRECIATION TO THE 2021-2022 MAYOR’S YOUTH 
COUNCIL AND STATE OF THE YOUTH ADDRESS.  
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda . Upon request by  Mr. Laybourn and pursuant to support 
received by Mr. Roybal  and Mr. Johnson, items #26, 36(a) and 36(c) were removed from the 
Consent Agenda.  Upon request by Mr. White, and pursuant to support received by Mr. Esquibel 
and Mr. Roybal, agenda item #37(a) was removed from the Consent Agenda.   Dr. Aldrich moved 
to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body present.   
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on May 9 , 202 2.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for a bar & grill liquor license application filed with 
the City Clerk’s Office for Westby Edge LLC d/b/a Westby Edge LLC, 714 W. 20th St., Cheyenne, 
WY.  Public comment was made by Misha Westby.  There being no further comments or input 
and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public hearing and referred 
the application to Finance Committee. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a p ublic hearing for a microbrewery license application filed with 
the City Clerk’s Office for Westby Edge LLC d/b/a Westby Edge LLC, 714 W. 20th St., Cheyenne, 
WY.  There being no  comments or input and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor 
Collins closed the public hearing and referred the application to Finance Committee. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to receive comments and input for the Hynds and 
the Hole Urban Renewal Plan and Project as required by Wyoming Statute pursuant to Wyoming 
Statute §15-9-109.  Public comment was made by Alex Davison, Patton & Davison, LLC. There 
being no further comment or input, and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins 
closed the public hearing. 
 Mayor Collins opened a p ublic hearing to receive comments a nd input for the 15 th Street 
Corridor Renewal Plan and Project as required by Wyoming Statute pursuant to Wyoming Statute 
§15-9-109.  Comment was made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich 
and Mr. Laybourn. There being no comments or input and purpose of the hearing having been met, 
Mayor Collins closed the public hearing.

 Bar and grill license  application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Billy Jacks – 
Wyoming LLC d/b/a Billy Jack’s Pizza Pub, 4020 Airport Parkway West Suite 1, Cheyenne, WY.  
Public comment was made by Jerry Cox, Director of Operations for Billy Jack’s Pizza Pub.   Mr. 
White moved to approve, seconded by Mr . Roybal.  Mr. White moved to postpone to June 13, 
2022, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion to postpone carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body present. 
 
[CA] Microbrewery license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Blue Raven 
Brewery, LLC, d/b/a Blue Raven Brewery, LLC, 209 E. 18 th St., Cheyenne, WY.   Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
Public comment was made by Jim Ridgeway on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
No comments were made on the Revenue Report as presented. 
 
Appointment of Jason M. Sanchez as Community Recreation and Events Director for the 
City of Cheyenne effective July 6, 2022.  Mr. Roybal moved to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  
Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich and Mayor 
Collins.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body  present with the 
exception of Mr. Johnson voting “no” and Mayor Collins who did not vote (mayoral appointment). 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held June 13, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
for a bar and grill liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for SAF Compliance 
LLC d/b/a Paris West Restaurant & Bar, 1719 Central Ave., Cheyenne, WY.   
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held June 13, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
for a proposed trade of real property owned by the City of Cheyenne, described as a portion of 
Missile Drive, Block 1, consisting of 2.35 acres and having an appraised value of $547,658.00, to 
Retail Properties LLC, for real property owned by Retail Properties LLC, described as a portion 
of Grier Leech Addition, 4th Filing, Lot 4, Block 9, consisting of 2.89 acres and having an appraised 
value of $548,083.00.     
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held June 13, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City 
Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference meeting, 
for a proposed trade of real property owned by the City  of Cheyenne, described as Storey Blvd. 
Extension:  Lot 1, Block 6, consisting of 2.24 acres and having an appraised value of $86,000.00, 
and 1720 Cleveland Ave., WYNN Addition:  Lot 1, Block 1, consisting of 2.18 acres and having 
an appraised value of $1,040,000.00, for real property owned by US 30 BP LLC, described as US 
30 Business Plaza, 2 nd Filing:  Lot 1, Block 1, consisting of 2.86 acres and having an appraised 
value of $1,350,000.00.       
   
 The Following members of the governing body made comments under other business  – 
Mr. White, Mayor Collins and Mr. Esquibel.  
 
 There being  no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:47 p.m.

        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk

June 13, 2022 
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich (via Zoom), Bryan Cook, Ken 
Esquibel (via Zoom), Richard Johnson,  Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal , Tom Segrave and Jeff 
White. Absent: Pete Laybourn. Also present: Kristina F. Jones , City Clerk.   The pledge of 
allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. Upon request by Mr. Cook and pursuant to support received 
by Mr. Johnson and Mr. Roybal, items #23 and 27 were removed from the Consent Agenda.  Mr. 
Segrave moved to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. Johnson.  Motion carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body present.

following members of the governing body: Mr. Johnson.   There being no further comments or 
input and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the public hearing and 
referred the application to Finance Committee. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a public hearing to receive comments and input for a proposed trade 
of real property owned by the City of Cheyenne, described as a portion of Missile Drive, Block 1,

members of the governing body.

WY – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
No comments were made on the Revenue Report as presented. 
 
 
 
 
 
Reappointments of Anthony Ortiz to the Cheyenne Downtown Development Authority; 
Chet Halvorson to the Urban Renewal Authority; and Julie Torres, Danny Glick, and Beth Lance 
to the Laramie County Community Juvenile Services Joint Powers Board.  Mr. Segrave moved to 
approve, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body with the exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote (mayoral appointments). 
 
Appointments of Chad Dunlay to the Laramie County Community Juvenile Services Joint 
Powers Board; Darin Westby to the City/County Frontier Park Improvements Joint Powers Board; 
Meghan Connor to the Planning Commission; and Josh Morris to the Cheyenne -Laramie County 
Economic Development Joint Powers Board. Mr.  Segrave moved to approve, seconded by Mr.  
Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of 
Mayor Collins who did not vote (mayoral appointments).  Comments were made by the following 
members of the governing body: Mayor Collins and Dr. Rinne. 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held July 11, 2022, at 6:00 P.M. in 
City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference 
meeting, for proposed Specific Purpose Sales Tax (6 th Penny) Projects to be completed in the 
Downtown Development District.  Prior to the public hearing, a work session with the City Council 
will be held on July 8, 2022, at 12:00 p.m. in City Council Chambers, and via electronic conference 
meeting, to outline the proposed projects. 
 
Announcement was made that a Calcutta benefitting Cheyenne area charities will be held 
on Saturday, July 30, 2022 beginning at 5:30 p.m. in the Cowboy Bistro, located beside the Rodeo 
Committee Cabin at Frontier Park, Cheyenne, WY, in conjunction with the 2022 Cheyenne 
Frontier Days Rodeo (10% of proceeds go to bona fide charities). 
 
Announcement was made that due to City offices being closed for the Independence Day 
holiday on July 4, 2022, regularly scheduled City Council Committee meetings will instead be 
held in the Municipal Building, Room 1 04, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, W Y, and via 
electronic conference meetings as follows:  Finance Committee will meet Tuesday, July 5, 2022 
at Noon and Public Services Committee will meet Wednesday, July 6, 2022 at Noon. 
 
Public comment was made by Jim Ridgeway. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business – 
Mayor Collins, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn, Mr. Segrave, Mr. White.  
  
 Mr. Roybal moved to meet in executive session pursuant to Wyoming Statute §16-4-
405(a)(vii), seconded by Mr.  Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session, not open 
to the public and stated there would be no action taken by the govern ing body as a result of the 
executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially conclude at the close 
of the executive session. The following staff members were in attendance:  City Attorney, City 
Clerk, City Treasurer, Chief of St aff and members of Planning and Development . (Clerk’s Note: 
executive session convened at 6:57 p.m.)  
 
 There being  no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:13 p.m.   
 
 
        Submitted by, 
 
             
  
        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk 
 
This notice is available in alternative, accessible formats upon request. 
 
Published: Wyoming Tribune-Eagle 
  July 6, 2022

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
July 11, 2022 
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich (via Zoom), Bryan Cook, Ken 
Esquibel, Richard Johnson,  Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne  (via Zoom) , Scott Roybal , Tom 
Segrave and Jeff White. Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was 
recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda . Mr. Segrave moved to approve , seconded by Mr. White.  
Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing bod y with the exception of Mr. 
Laybourn voting “no”. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on June 27, 2022.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a public hearing for proposed Specific Purpose Sales Tax (6 th 
Penny) Projects to be completed in the Downtown Development District.  Comments were made 
by the following members of the governing body: Mr. Segrave.  There being no further comments 
or input and purpose of the hearing having been met, Mayor Collins closed the hearing. 
 
 ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending Unified Development Code Section 1.4.4.f, 
Industrial Services Uses, to create a definition for Data Center and to amend definitions for 
clarification and amending Table 5-1, Zoning District Uses, of Unified Development Code Section 
5.1.4, Districts and Uses, to classify the Permissibility of Data Center .  Reporting for Public 
Services Committee, Mr. Cook moved to approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. 
Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. (#4450) 
 
 ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending Section 9.08.010(B)(3), Disturbance of the 
Peace and Disturbance of Property, Chapter 9.08, Offenses Against Public Peace and Decency, of 
Title 9, Public Peace and Welfare, of  the Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming .  Mr. Cook 
moved to approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body. (#4451) 
 
 ORDINANCE – 2nd READING – Amending Section 2.40.020, Appointment of Judges – 
Powers Generally, of Chapter 2.40, Municipal Court, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, of 
the Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming Mr. Cook moved to approve on second reading, 
seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  
Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Mr. Johnson and Mr. 
Segrave.     
 
 ORDINANCE – 2nd READING – Amending various sections of Chapter 5.12, A lcoholic 
Liquor and Malt Beverages, of Title 5, Business Licenses and Regulations, of the Code of the City 
of Cheyenne, Wyoming .  Reporting for Finance Committee, Mr. White  moved to approve on 
second reading, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Comments were made by the following members of the 
governing body: Mayor Collins and Mr. Johnson.     
 
 ORDINANCE – 1st READING – Amending various sections of Chapter 13.12, Sewer 
System, of Title 13, Public Services, of the Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming to 
allow for exceptions to sewage disposal requirements  – referred to Public Services Committee.  
(Sponsor – Mr. Cook) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Authorizing the submission of an application by the City of Cheyenne 
for federal funding through the Transportation Alternatives Program administered by the 
Wyoming Department of Transportation for the purpose of the con struction of two (2) high 
intensity activated cross walk systems located across Western Hills Boulevard at McCormick 
Junior High School and across Pershing Boulevard at McCann Avenue.   Motion (per Consent 
Agenda) carried.  (#6255) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Acknowledging receipt of the East Cheyenne Community Park Master 
Plan Report and Appendices – referred to Public Services Committee. (Sponsors – Mr. Esquibel 
& Mr. Johnson) 
 
[CA]  Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Warehouse Twenty 
One for the Destination Wayfinding Signage Plan, Phase III Fabrication and Installation Services 
Project (Specific Purpose Sales Tax and Greenway Donations).   Motion (per Consent Agenda) 
carried (in an amount not to exceed $29,185.00). (#7548) 
 
[CA]  Industrial and Economic Development Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne 
and the Cheyenne/Laramie County Corporation for Economic Development (LEADS) (General 
Fund).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in an amount not to exceed $75,000.00). (#7549) 
 
[CA]  Parking Lot Lease Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Riverstone Bank for the 
lease of parking spaces at 1716 Thomes Avenue (General Fund).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) 
carried (in an amount not to exceed $14,416.80). (#7550) 
 
 Purchase and sale agreement for real property between Retail Properties C, LLC and the 
City of Cheyenne for the acquisition of land for a future fire station location (General Fund).  Mr. 
White moved to approve in an amount not to exceed $548,082.60, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Mr. 
White moved to amend by substitute dated July 1, 2022, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to

[CA]  Agreement for the purchase, sale and exchange of lands between the City of Cheyenne and

Memorandum of Agreement between the City of Cheyenne, Reiman Corporation and AVI,

Reconstruction Project (2019-2022 One Percent Sales Tax) .  Mr. White moved to approve,

(#7553)

[CA]  Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne, Cheyenne Metropolitan 
Planning Organization (MPO) and A.V.I. p.c. for the Powderhouse Road Corridor Plan (MPO 
Federal Funds) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA]  Agreement between the City of  Cheyenne and the Cheyenne Animal Shelter for Animal 
Shelter Services (General Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
          
 Contract Modification #1  to Contract #7461 between the City of Cheyenne, Cheyenne 
Metropolitan Planning Organization (MPO), and West Edge Collective, LLC, for Public 
Engagement and Web Support Services (MPO Federal Funds). Mr. White moved to approve in an 
amount not to exceed $5,000.00, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body. (#7461)  
 
[CA] Contract Modification #2  to Contract #7287  between the City of Cheyenne, Cheyenne 
Metropolitan Planning Organization (MPO), and Benchmark Engineers, PC, for the Walterscheid 
Boulevard Reconstruction Plan (MPO Federal Funds).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in 
an amount not to exceed $4,800.00). (#7287) 
                        
[CA] Contract Modification #2  to Contract #6445 between the City of Cheyenne and AVI 
Professional Corporation for additional design services for the 17 th Street Lighting Project 
(Specific Purpose Sales Tax Overage Funds).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in an amount 
not to exceed $11,125.00). (#6445) 
 
[CA] Consideration of Bid #E -30-22 for as -needed emergency snow control for the City of 
Cheyenne, Public Works Department (2019-2022 1% Sales Tax).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) 
carried (accepted bid from JTL Group Inc. d/b/a Knife River, Cheyenne, in an amount not to 
exceed $300,000.00). 
 
 Request to expand seating area for consumption relating to City of Cheyenne microbrewery 
liquor license held by Freedom’s Edge Brewing Co., located at 1509 Pioneer Avenue, Cheyenne, 
WY.  Mr. White moved to approve contingent upon City Engineering, Building, and Planning & 
Development requirements being met, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – 
all members of the governing body. 
 
Public comment was made by Jim Ridgeway on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held July 25, 2022, at 6:0 0 P.M. in 
City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference 
meeting, for a restaurant liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Los 
Aguacates Inc. d/b/a Tres Amigo Family Mexican Restaurant, 1802  Dell Range Blvd., Suite 2, 
Cheyenne, WY.    
 
Public comment was made by Jim Ridgeway under other business. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business – Dr. 
Aldrich, Mayor Collins, Mr. Cook and Mr. Johnson.  
  
 There being  no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 6:48 p.m.

        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk

Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne , Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White .

Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda . Upon request by Mr.  Laybourn and pursuant to support

Powers Generally, of Chapter 2.40, Municipal Court, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, of

Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  Comments were made by the following 
members of the governing body: Dr. Rinne.  (#4452)  
 
 ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending various sections of Chapter 5.12, Alcoholic

 ORDINANCE – 2nd READING – Amending various sections of Chapter 13.12, Sewer 
System, of Title 13, Public Services, of the Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming to 
allow for exceptions to sewage disposal requirements .  Dr. Rinne moved to approve on second 
reading, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried. Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body. 
 
 ORDINANCE – 1st READING – Amending Ordinance No. 4411 Defining the Terms and 
Conditions of Employment for sworn officers of the Cheyenne Police Department for Fiscal Year 
2022-2023 – referred to Finance Committee. (Sponsor – Mr. White) 
 
ORDINANCE – 1st READING – Amending Chapter 1.16, Annexation and Infrastructure 
Improvements, of Title 1, General Provisions, of the Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, 
Wyoming, to repeal annexation provisions, amend definitions, and modify processes – referred to 
Public Services Committee.  (Sponsor – Mr. Cook) 
 
 RESOLUTION – Acknowledging receipt of the East Cheyenne Community Park Master 
Plan Report and Appendices .  Dr. Rinne moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion 
carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of th e governing body.   Comments were made by the 
following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Laybourn. (#6256) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Supporting the Cheyenne Regional Airport Board in the application for 
and acceptance of federal assistance from the Federal Aviation Administration under the Airport 
Improvement Program to assist in funding costs related to a reimbursable agreement for Precision 
Approach Path Indicator and Localizer Design and Construction – referred to Finance Committee. 
(Sponsor – Mr. White) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Authorizing the submission of an application to the State Loan and 
Investment Board for a grant through Water and Sewer ARPA Grant Program Funding on behalf 
of the Governing Body for the City of Cheyenne for the purpose of providing critically needed 
flood control at East Dell Range Boulevard and Van Buren Avenue – referred to Finance 
Committee. (Sponsor – Mr. White) 
 
 Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and HDR Engineering, 
Inc. for the Pedestrian Traffic Safety Study and HAWK System Design Project (2019-2022 1% 
Sales Tax). Mr. White moved to approve in an amount not to exceed $73,271.00, seconded by Mr. 
Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.   Comments were 
made by the following members of the governing body: Mr. Laybourn. (#7561) 
 
[CA]  Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne, Cheyenne M etropolitan 
Planning Organization (MPO) and A.V.I. p.c. for the Powderhouse Road Corridor Plan (MPO 
Federal Funds).  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried (in an amount not to exceed $147,694.80). 
(#7562) 
 
 Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and the Cheyenne Animal Shelter for Animal 
Shelter Services (General Fund).   Mr. White moved to approve in an amount not to exceed 
$528,000.00, seconded by Mr.  Roybal.  Mr. Cook moved to amend by substitute dated July 18, 
2022, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to amend by substitute failed.  Voting “no” – all members 
of the governing body.  Dr. Aldrich moved to postpone to August 8, 2022, seconded by Mr. 
Segrave.  Motion to postpone carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.   
Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mayor 
Collins, Mr. Cook, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn and Dr. Rinne. 
 
[CA] Agreement between the City of Cheyenne, Civic Center, and I.A.T.S.E. Local 229 for 
production services (Civic Center Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Plan One Architects for architectural 
services for the Cheyenne Solid Waste Transfer Station original building roof replacement (Solid 
Waste Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
                                               
[CA]  Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and Patrick Engineering 
Inc., for the Inventory, Condition Assessment, and Data Migration of City Facility a ssets to 
Cartegraph OMS Project (American Rescue Plan Federal Recovery Funds ) – referred to Finance 
Committee. 
 
[CA]  Professional Services Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and KLJ Engineering for 
the Environmental Permitting Services for the City of Cheyenne’s industrial facilities (Solid Waste 
Fund & 2019-2022 1% Sales Tax) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA]  Lease Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and the Cheyenne Airport Board for land 
to be used for Community Recreation and Events softball and baseball fields (2019-2022 1% Sales 
Tax) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA]  Lease Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and the Cheyenne Airport Board for land 
to be used for Street and Alley for Compost operations (20 19-2022 1% Sales Tax) – referred to 
Finance Committee. 
 
[CA]  Lease Agreement between the City of Cheyenne and the Cheyenne Airport Board for land 
to be used for the Weed and Pest Division  (2019-2022 1% Sales Tax)  – referred to Finance 
Committee. 
 
 Contract Modification #1 to Contract #7255 between the City of Cheyenne and Simon 
Contractors for the East 12 th Street Meadow Drive Mill and Overlay Project for the addition of 
storm sewer improvements (1% Specific Purpose Sales Tax) .  Mr. White advised the item was 
reviewed under other items for the agenda at the July 18, 2022 Finance Committee meeting, 
therefore, provisions of Title 2 of City Cod e have been met.  Mr. White moved to approve in an 
amount not to exceed $272,462.60, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body.  Comments were made by the following members of the 
governing body: Dr. Aldrich. (#7255) 
 
[CA] Request to expand seating area for consumption relating to City of Ch eyenne Bar & Grill 
Liquor License held by Paris West Restaurant and Bar, located at 1719 Central Avenue, Cheyenne, 
WY – referred to Finance Committee. 
Reappointments of Miles Henderson and Scott Thomas to the Public Transit Advisory 
Board. Mr. Roybal moved to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – 
all members of the governing body with the exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote (Mayoral 
appointments).    
 
Appointments of Richard Leslie to the Public Transit Advisory Board; Shane Paul to the 
Mayor’s Council for People with Disabilities; and Amy Hernandez to the Planning Commission.  
Mr. Roybal moved to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body with the exceptio n of Mayor Collins who did not vote (Mayoral 
appointments).   
 
Comment was made by Dr. Aldrich on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
No comments were made on the revenue report as presented. 
 
Under other business: 
 
Mr. White noted the motion to approve the purchase and sale agreement between the City 
of Cheyenne and Retail Properties C, LLC passed at the July 11, 2022 City Council meeting.  
Having voted on the prevailing side and pursuant to Chapter 2.04 of City Code, Mr. White moved 
to reconsider the vote taken on the purchase and sale agreement, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion 
to reconsider carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  Mr. Segrave moved to 
amend by substitute, seconded by Mr.  Roybal.  Motion to amend by substitute carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body.  Main motion as amended by substitute carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body. Comments were made by the following members of 
the governing body: Mr. Segrave. (#7551) 
 
 There being  no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 6:33 p.m.   
 
        Submitted by, 
 
             
  
        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY

August 08, 2022 
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne  (via Zoom), Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and 
Jeff White. Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda Upon request by Dr. Aldrich and pursuant to support received 
by Mr. White and Mr. Johnson, item #16, was removed from the Consent Agenda.  Upon request 
by Mr. White and pursuant to support received by Mr. Esquibel and Mr. Johnson, items #25(f), 
#25(g) and #25(h) were removed from the Consent Agenda. President Roybal moved to approve 
remaining items, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing bod y. [Clerk’s note:  Mr. Cook arrived during discussion prior to vote of Consent 
Agenda items]

Consent Agenda) carried.

allow for exceptions to sewage disposal requirements.  Reporting for the Public Services 
Committee, Mr. Segrave moved to approve on third and final reading, seconded by Mr. White. 
Mr. Segrave moved to postpone until August 22, 2022 , seconded by Mr. Cook.  Motion carried.

Powers Board; Aaron Bentley to the Revolving Loan Fund Committee; and Richard Leslie and 
Ramon Moya to the Housing and Community Development Advisory Council.  Mr. Roybal moved 
to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body with the exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote (Mayoral appointments). 
 
 Reappointments of Dominic Valdez to the Revolving Loan Fund Committee; and Stephen 
Ganison to the Housing and Community Development Advisory Council.  Mr . Roybal moved to 
approve, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body with the exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote (Mayoral appointments). 
  
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
No comments were made on the Revenue report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made that due to City offices being closed for the Labor Day holiday 
on Monday, September 5, 2022, regularly scheduled City Council Committee meetings will 
instead be held in the Municipal Building, Room 104, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, W Y, and 
via electronic conference meetings as follows:  Finance Committee will meet Tuesday, September 
6, 2022 at Noon and Public Services Committee will meet Wednesday, September 7, 2022 at Noon 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business : Dr. 
Aldrich, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White. 
 
Mr. Segrave moved to meet in executive session pursuant to Wyoming Statute §16 -4-
405(a)(iii), seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session, not open to the public 
and stated there would be no action taken by the governing body as a result of the executive 
session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially conclude at the close of the 
executive session. The following staff members were in attendance: City Attorney and City Clerk.  
(Clerk’s Note: executive session convened at 6:50 p.m.) 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:09 p.m.   
 
        Submitted by, 
 
             
  
        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
September 12, 2022 
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson,  Dr. Mark Rinne , Scott Roybal , Tom Segrave  and Jeff White .  Absent: Pete 
Laybourn.  Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda . Mr. Roybal moved to approve , seconded by Mr. White.  
Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on August 22, 2022.  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried.

meeting, therefore, provisions of Title 2 of City Code had been met.  Mr. White moved to approve, 
seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body 
present. Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Mr. Cook. 
(#7199) 
 
[CA] Amendment #2 to  Contract #5939 between the City of Cheyenne and The Lamar 
Companies for billboard leases (Revenue to the City) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Contract Modification #1 to Contract #7488 between the City of Cheyenne and MHP, LLP 
to provide financial and compliance auditing services for the City of Cheyenne and the Cheyenne 
Board of Public Utilities for fiscal year ending June 30, 2022 (American Rescue Plan Funds) – 
referred to Finance Committee. 
 
[CA] Contract Modification #1 to Contract #7504  between the City of Cheyenne and FCI 
Constructors of Wyoming LLC for the Emergency Response Facilities Guaranteed Maximum 
Price (GMP) (2021 Specific Purpose Option Tax Fund and 2017 Specific Purpose Option Tax 
Fund Overages) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
 
[CA] Consideration to purchase between the City of Cheyenne and Econolite, for four (4) traffic 
controller cabinets for the City of Cheyenne Traffic Division (2019-2022 Optional 1% Sales Tax 
Fund) – referred to Finance Committee. 
 
 Appointments of Annalycia  Aguirres, William Barrington, Julianne Beech, Ty Bronder, 
Katelyn Cobb, Kaedyn Patrick Austin Davenport, Nora Fraley, Caitlyn Ganskow, Heidi Glaze, 
Nevaeh Green, Kristen Huffman, Madalyn Huffman, Andrew Lance, Molly Madsen and Elizabeth 
Stump to the 2022 -2023 Mayor’s Youth Council.  Mr. Segrave moved to approve, seconded by 
Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present with the 
exception of Mayor Collins who did not vote (mayoral appointments).  Comments were made by 
the following members of the governing body: Mayor Collins. 
  
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made  that a Calcutta, benefitting the Wyoming Breast Ca ncer 
Initiative, will be held Friday, September 30 – Sunday, October 2, 2022, beginning at 5:00 p.m. 
each night at Peppermill Bar, 1618 Stillwater Ave., Cheyenne, Wyoming.   
 
Public comment was made by Jim Ridgeway under other business. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business – Dr. 
Aldrich, Mr. Johnson and Mr. White.  
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 7:13 p.m.   
 
        Submitted by, 
 
             
  
        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk

September 26, 2022 
 
 The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich , Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne , Tom Segrave and Jeff White .  Absent: Scott 
Roybal.  Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
 Mayor Collins announced the legal advertisement published on September 23, 2022 in the 
Wyoming Tribune-Eagle related to the public hearing for a proposed  trade of property between 
the City of Cheyenne and Mark Eisele contained a typographical date error and advised the public 
hearing will be held on October 10, 2022 as announced on the agenda. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. Upon request by Dr. Rinne and pursuant to support received 
by Mr. Cook and Mr. Esquibel, item #17 was removed from the Consent Agenda .  Upon request 
by Mr. Cook and pursuant to support received by  Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Johnson, item # 18 was 
removed from the Consent Agenda.  Upon request by Dr. Aldrich and pursuant to support received

by Mr. White and pursuant to support received by Mr.  Cook and Mr. Esquibel, item #25(a) was 
removed from the Consent Agenda .  Mr. Johnson moved to approve remaining items, seconded 
by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.

the governing body present.  Motion to amend by substitute as amended carried.   Voting “yes” – 
all members of the governing body present.   Main motion as amended by substitute as amended 
carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.   Public comment was made

amount not to exceed $39,160.00).

Netting Mountain West for the Happy Jack Landfill Litter Fence Repair Project  (Solid Waste

 Report of Core Development Incentivization Program activities, park fees, and public

seconded by Mr . White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body 
present.   
 
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
 Announcement was made that a Calcutta, benefitting the Make-A-Wish Foundation, will 
be held Friday, November 11, 2022, beginning at 5:30 p.m. at Red Lion, 204 W. Fox Farm Rd., 
Cheyenne, Wyoming. 
 
Public comment was made by Donn Edmunds, Rick Poach, Susan Edgerton, Billy 
Richards, Sarah Meyer, Marilyn Burden and Marla Richards under other business. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business : Dr. 
Aldrich, Mayor Collins and Mr. Johnson.  
 
Mr. Roybal moved to meet in executive session pursuant to W yoming Statute §16 -4-
405(a)(x), seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body present.  Mayor Collins announced members would meet in executive session, not open to 
the public and stated there would be no actio n taken by the governing body as a result of the 
executive session, and that the regular City Council meeting would officially conclude at the close 
of the executive session. The following staff members were in attendance: City Attorney, City 
Clerk, Fire Chief, Mayor ’s Chief of Staff and Deputy City Attorney .  (Clerk’s Note: executive 
session convened at 8:02 p.m.) 
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 8:26 p.m.   
 
        Submitted by, 
 
             
  
        Kristina F. Jones,   
City Clerk

Cheyenne Traffic Division (2019 -2022 Optional 1% Sales Tax Fund) – referred to Finance 
Committee. 
 
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held November 28, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. 
in City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference 
meeting, to determine whether compliance of annexation conditions required by Wyoming Statute 
§15-1-402 exist for about 0.36 acres of land at 4612 Van Buren Avenue, Laramie County, 
Wyoming (located west of Van Buren Avenue, north of Dell Range Boulevard).

in City Council Ch ambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, and via electronic conference 
meeting, for a transfer of ownership of retail liquor license application filed with the City Clerk’s 
Office for Andiamo 307 LLC d/b/a Andiamo 307, 1808 O’Neil Ave., Cheyenne, WY (lice nse 
currently held by Poor Richard’s d/b/a Poor Richard’s).        
 
Public comment was made by John Taylor and Madeline Martinez under other business. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business : Dr. 
Aldrich, Mr. Cook, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Laybourn and Mr. White.  
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 8:02 p.m.

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY

November 28, 2022 
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook, Richard Johnson, 
Pete Laybourn, Dr. Mark Rinne,  Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White. Absent:  Ken 
Esquibel. Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the de signation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda .  Mr. Roybal moved to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  
Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on November 14, 2022.  Motion 
(per Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
 Mayor Collins opened a Public Hearing  to determine whether compliance of annexation 
conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15-1-402 exist for about 0.36 acres of land at 4612 Van 
Buren Avenue, Laramie County, Wyoming (located west of Van Buren Avenue, north of Dell 
Range Boulevard).  Purpose of the hearing having been  met, Mayor Collins closed the public 
hearing.  Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich. 
 
Mayor Collins opened a Public Hearing for a transfer of ownership of retail liquor license 
application filed with the City Clerk’s Office for Andiamo 307 LLC d/b/a Andiamo 307, 1808 
O’Neil Ave., Cheyenne, WY (license currently held by Poor Richard’s d/b/a Poor Richard’s).

made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Roybal and Mr. Segrave.

container as specified.  Reporting for Public Services Committee, Dr. Rinne advised there was no

seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to amend by substitute carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of

ORDINANCE – 2nd READING Amending Ordinance #4447, approving and establishing

Reporting for Finance Committee, Mr. White moved to approve on second reading, seconded by

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
December 12, 2022 
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Dr. Mark Rinne, Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White. Absent: Pete 
Laybourn.  Also present: Kylie Soden, Deputy City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
 Mayor Collins announced that agenda item #28(h), Memorandum of Understanding 
between the City of Cheyenne and Dozzer, LLC, to authorize filing of platting application(s) for 
City-owned lands for the purpose of access and future development, would be remove d from the 
agenda at the request of staff and the developer. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda.  Upon request by Mr. White and pursuant to support received 
by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Cook, item #24(e) was removed from the Consent Agenda.  Mr. Segrave 
moved to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all 
members of the governing body present. 
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on November 28, 2022.  Motion 
(per Consent Agenda) carried.

committee.  Mr. White moved to approve, seconded by Dr. Aldrich.  Dr. Aldrich moved to amend 
by including a reversion clause that if the license is not in use by March 31, 2023 it will be reverted 
back to the city, seconded by Mr. Segrave.  Motion to amend failed.  Voting “yes” – Dr. Aldrich, 
Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Segrave and Mr. White.  Voting “no” – Mayor Collins, Mr. Cook, Mr. Johnson, 
Dr. Rinne and Mr. Roybal.  Main motion failed. Voting “yes” – Mayor Collins, Mr. Cook, Dr. 
Rinne and Mr. Roybal.  Voting “no” – Dr. Aldrich, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Mr. Segrave and 
Mr. White. Comments were made by the following members of the governing body: Dr. Aldrich, 
Mr. Cook, Mr. Esquibel, Mr. Johnson, Dr. Rinne, and Mr. Segrave. 
                                      
No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
Announcement was made that a Calcutta benefitting the Gold Star Family Charity, will be 
held January 13 - January 15, 2023 beginning at 4:00 p.m. each day at American Legion Post 6, 
2001 E. Lincolnway, Cheyenne, Wyoming (10% of proceeds go to the charity). 
 
Announcement was made that a Committee of the Whole meeting will be held on 
December 21, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, 
and via electronic conference meeting. 
 
Announcement was made that due to the Christmas holiday schedule, the regularly 
scheduled City Council meeting on Monday, December 26, 2022, will instead be held on Tuesday, 
December 27, 2022 at 6:00 p.m. in City Council Chambers, 2101 O’Neil Avenue, Cheyenne, WY, 
and via electronic conference meeting. 
 
Public comment was made by Jim Barnes under other business. 
 
The following members of the governing body made comments under other business : Dr. 
Aldrich and Mr. Johnson.  
 
 There being no further business to come before the governing body, the meeting was 
officially adjourned at 6:50 p.m.   
 
        Submitted by, 
 
             
  
        Kylie Soden,    
Deputy City Clerk, CMC

MEETING: City Council Meeting | DATE: 2022-12-27 | CLIP_ID: 877 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 877

RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
December 27, 2022  
  
The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:00 p.m. in Council Chambers and via electronic conference meeting.  Present were: MAYOR 
– Patrick Collins, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Dr. Michelle Aldrich, Bryan Cook,  Ken Esquibel, 
Richard Johnson, Pete Laybourn. Dr. Mark Rinne,  Scott Roybal, Tom Segrave and Jeff White . 
Also present: Kristina F. Jones, City Clerk.  The pledge of allegiance was recited. 
 
Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda .  Upon request by Mr. White , and pursuant to support 
received by Dr. Aldrich and Mr. Roybal, item #19 was removed from the Consent Agenda.  Mr. 
Roybal moved to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body.

(per Consent Agenda) carried.
